2%) which was referred to long-term growth rate of tourism
in Niseko and the long-term inflation rate in Japan.

shareholder (for enterprises), such as the type and value of        有的資產類型及價值。
assets owned by the potential borrower.

borrower and the financial condition of the borrower.

shareholders. Accordingly, the Company has adopted sound           之內部監控及對全體股東的問責性。
corporate governance principles that emphasize an effective
internal control and accountability to all shareholders.

DIVIDEND POLICY                                                   股息政策
The Board has adopted a dividend policy (the “Dividend            董 事 會 已 採 用 股 息 政 策（「股 息 政 策」）。股
Policy”). A summary of the Dividend Policy is disclosed as        息政策概要披露如下。
below.

1.   The Company may declare and pay dividends to the             1.   本公司可透過現金方式或董事會認為
     shareholders of the Company by way of cash or by                  適當的其他方式向本公司股東宣派及
     other means that the Board considers appropriate. It is           派付股息。推薦派付股息乃董事會的
     the policy of the Board, in recommending dividends, to            政策，旨在讓股東分享本公司溢利，
     allow the shareholders to participate in the Company’s            並同時確保本公司留存充足儲備作未
     profits, and at the same time, to ensure the Company              來發展。本公司於未來宣派或派付任
     to retain adequate reserves for future growth. The                何股息之決定及該股息之金額取決於
     Company’s decision to declare or to pay any dividends             （其中包括）現時及將來營運、財務狀
     in the future, and the amount of such dividends will              況、流動資金狀況及本集團資本所需
     depend upon, among other things, the current and                  以及本公司附屬公司及聯營公司收取
     future operations, financial condition, liquidity position        之股息，該股息取決於該等附屬公司
     and capital requirements of the Group, as well as                 及聯營公司派付股息之能力。此外，
     dividends received from the Company’s subsidiaries                就財政年度派付任何末期股息須經股
     and associates, which in turn will depend on the ability          東 批 准 後，方 可 作 實。本 公 司 宣 派 及
     of those subsidiaries and associates to pay a dividend.           派付股息亦須遵守百慕達法律、香港
     In addition, any final dividends for a financial year will        法例、本公司細則及任何適用法律、
     be subject to the approval of the shareholders. The               規則及規例項下任何限制。
     declaration and payment of dividends by the Company
     is also subject to any restrictions under the laws of
     Bermuda, the laws of Hong Kong, the Company’s bye-
     laws and any applicable laws, rules and regulations.

2.   Whilst the Dividend Policy reflects the Board’s current      2.   股息政策反映董事會現時對本集團財
     views on the financial and cash-flow position of the              務及現金流量狀況之意見，而董事會
     Group, the Board will continue to review the Dividend             將持續不時檢討此股息政策，董事會
     Policy from time to time and the Board may exercise               亦可能於其認為合適及必要之任何時
     its sole and absolute discretion to update, amend and/            間行使全部及絕對酌情權更新、修訂
     or modify the Dividend Policy at any time as it deems             及╱或 修 改 股 息 政 策。概 不 保 證 將 為
     fit and necessary. There is no assurance that dividends           任何特定期間宣派或派付任何特定金
     will be declared or paid in any particular amount                 額之股息。股息政策絕不構成本公司
     for any given period. The Dividend Policy shall in no             就派付任何金額股息作出之具法律約
     way constitute a legally binding commitment by the                束力承諾及╱或絕不強制本公司於任

     amount and/or in no way obligate the Company to

The Board ensures the maintenance of sound and effective          董 事 會 確 保 維 持 穩 健 有 效 之 內 部 監 控，以

and the assets of the Group. It has been an important duty of     持續檢討本集團內部監控及風險管理制度

system of internal control and risk management of the Group       包 括 財 務、營 運 及 合 規 監 控 以 及 風 險 管 理
on an on-going basis. The review covers all material controls,    職 能。風 險 管 理 及 內 部 監 控 制 度 旨 在 管 理
including financial, operational and compliance controls,         而 非 消 除 未 能 達 成 業 務 目 標 的 風 險，並 僅
as well as risk management functions. The systems of risk         能合理但非絕對保證並無重大錯誤陳述或
management and internal control are designed to manage            損失。
rather than eliminate the risk of failure in achieving business
objectives, and can only provide reasonable and not absolute
assurance against material misstatements or loss.

REPORTING STANDARDS AND                                          報告標準及原則
PRINCIPLES
This Report is in compliance with the reporting principles       本報告符合香港聯合交易所有限公司頒佈
determined by the ESG Reporting Code of contained in             的上市規則附錄 C2 所載環境、社會及管治
Appendix C2 to the Listing Rules issued by of The Stock          報 告 守 則 所 釐 定 的 報 告 原 則。此 外，報 告
Exchange of Hong Kong Limited. Besides, the reporting            原則詳述如下：
principles are detailed as follows:

Materiality              Through conducting a materiality assessment and engaging stakeholders of the
                         Group, the Board reviews and prioritizes the ESG issues that are most relevant to
                         the Group. Details of the stakeholder engagement can be found in the “Materiality
                         Matrix” section.
重要性                      透過重要性評估及本集團持份者之參與，董事會審視對本集團而言最相關之環境、
                         社會及管治事宜並確定該等事宜之優先次序。有關持份者參與之詳情可參閱「重
                         要性矩陣」一節。

Quantitative             This Report discloses the relevant standards and methodologies in the calculation
                         of environmental and social key performance indicators (“KPIs”) with the aim of
                         ensuring the data disclosed is measurable and comparable. Performance targets of
                         KPIs are set by the Group based on historical data and business development plan.
量化                       本報告披露計算環境及社會關鍵績效指標（「關鍵績效指標」）時所採用之相關標
                         準及方法，旨在確保所披露數據可計量及可資比較。本集團基於過往資料及業務
                         發展計劃設定關鍵績效指標之表現目標。

Balance                  This Report provides an unbiased picture of the ESG performance of the Group
                         during the Reporting Period by avoiding the selection, omission, or presentation
                         formats that may inappropriately influence the judgement of the readers.
平衡                       本報告避免使用可能對讀者判斷產生不當影響之篩選、遺漏或呈報方式，以公平
                         反映本集團於報告期間之環境、社會及管治表現。

Consistency              The data calculation methodologies of this Report shall be consistent to facilitate
                         comparisons with historical data.
一致性                      本報告使用之數據計算方法應一致，以利便與歷史數據進行比較。

The Group has adopted the principle of materiality in the ESG    本 集 團 已 於 環 境、社 會 及 管 治 報 告 守 則 中
Reporting Code and identified the key ESG issues that are        採 用 重 要 性 原 則，並 已 識 別 對 本 集 團 業 務
important to the business of the Group. All the key ESG areas,   而言屬重要的主要環境、社會及管治議題。
aspects and key performance indicators (KPIs) are reported       本 報 告 已 根 據 環 境、社 會 及 管 治 報 告 守 則
in this Report are according to recommendations of the ESG       的 推 薦 建 議，呈 報 所 有 主 要 環 境、社 會 及
Reporting Code.                                                  管治範疇、層面及關鍵績效指標。

     A2. Use of Resources                                      A2. 資源使用
        The Group strives to take all feasible measures          本集團致力採取一切可行措施，
        to incorporate sustainability into its business          將可持續發展融入其業務營運

        performance. Realising that resource conservation        節約資源對保持環境可持續發展
        is crucial for maintaining environmental                 至關重要，因此我們提倡綠色辦
        sustainability, we promote green office                  公室管理，並鼓勵僱員注意於日

       Step 1: Set Future Images Assuming Climate               第一步：根據氣候變化影響的假
       Change Effects                                           設組建未來景象
       As climate change measures proceed, there is             隨著氣候變化措施的推行，行業
       a possibility that the industry will be exposed          可能面臨重大變化，例如引入及
       to substantial changes, such as stricter policies        上調碳定價等更嚴格的政策，以
       including the introduction of and increases in           及技術進步及客戶觀念的變化。
       carbon pricing, as well as advances in technology
       and changes in customer awareness.

       In light of these climate change effects, based on       因應該等氣候變化的影響，我們
       the International Energy Agency (“IEA”) scenarios        按照國際能源署（「IEA」）發佈的
       and others, we developed multiple future images          情境及其他依據，組建了本集團

     The Group prohibits engagement of child and               本集團遵守（包括但不限於）香港

     Security Act of Japan (Act No. 141 of 1947). We           止聘用童工及強制勞動。我們已
     have implemented a preventive recruitment                 實施防範未然的招聘程序，並進
     procedure with a thorough background check,               行徹底的背景調查，確保不會僱
     to ensure that no underaged or illegal persons            用未成年人或非法勞工。此外，
     are employed. Furthermore, to prevent unlawful            為 防 止 非 法 招 聘 未 滿 15 歲 的 童

     child labour, employees are required to provide           必須向人力資源部提供身份證明
     identity proofs to Human Resources Department             以核實年齡。所有的工作均須自
     to verify the age as part of the recruitment              願而不得涉及強迫勞動。如有任
     process. All work should be voluntarily performed         何違反有關勞工準則的法律及法
     and shall not involve forced labour. If any violation     規之行為，我們定必徹查事件，
     against laws and regulations in relation to labour        視事件之嚴重程度而對負責員工
     standards is found, we will investigate the incident,     給予適當處分，並檢討現有人力
     impose appropriate penalty to accountable staff           資源系統是否有任何不足之處。
     subject to the severity and review any defects in         再者，本集團絕不以任何方式強
     the human resources system in place. Besides,             迫僱員超時工作。倘僱員無可避
     the Group does not in any way force its employees         免須加班，我們根據勞工法例及

     inevitable.

OPINION                                                            意見
We have audited the consolidated financial statements of 本核數師（以下簡稱「我們」）已審計載於第
DeTai New Energy Group Limited (the “Company”) and its 115 至 200 頁 之 德 泰 新 能 源 集 團 有 限 公 司
subsidiaries (together the “Group”) set out on pages 115 to （「貴公司」）及其附屬公司（統稱「貴集團」）
200, which comprise the consolidated statement of financial 之 綜 合 財 務 報 表，此 等 綜 合 財 務 報 表 包 括
position as at 30 June 2025, and the consolidated statement 於二零二五年六月三十日之綜合財務狀況

1.    GENERAL INFORMATION                                                           1.   一般資料
      DeTai New Energy Group Limited (the “Company”) is                                  德泰新能源集團有限公司（「本公司」）
      incorporated in Bermuda as an exempted company with                                在百慕達註冊成立為獲豁免有限公司，
      limited liability and its shares are listed on The Stock                           其股份在香港聯合交易所有限公司（「聯
      Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock Exchange”).                              交所」）上市。本公司註冊辦事處地址
      The addresses of the Company’s registered office is                                為 Clarendon House, 2 Church Street,
      Clarendon House, 2 Church Street, Hamilton HM 11,                                  Hamilton HM 11, Bermuda，而 其 主 要
      Bermuda and its principal place of business was Unit                               營業地點為香港九龍灣宏照道 19 號金
      D2, 8/F, Kingston International Centre, 19 Wang Chiu                               利豐國際中心 8 樓 D2 室。
      Road, Kowloon Bay, Hong Kong.

      The Company is an investment holding company. The                                  本公司為一家投資控股公司。本公司
      Company’s subsidiaries are principally engaged in the                              附 屬 公 司 之 主 要 業 務 為：(i) 酒 店 款 待
      business of (i) hotel hospitality business; (ii) provision of                      業務；(ii) 提供借貸服務；(iii) 買賣及分
      money lending services; (iii) trading and distribution of                          銷酒類產品；及 (iv) 上市證券投資。
      liquor and wine; and (iv) investments in listed securities.

      (a) Adoption of new or amendments to                                               (a) 自二零二四年七月一日起採

           standards, amendments and interpretations                                        公會」）頒佈之新訂準則、修訂及
           (“the new or amendments to HKFRS Accounting                                      詮釋（「新訂或經修訂香港財務報
           Standards”) issued by the Hong Kong Institute of                                 告準則會計準則」），該等新訂或
           Certified Public Accountants (“HKICPA”), which                                   經修訂香港財務報告準則會計準

           July 2024:                                                                       表生效：

            Interpretation 5 (Revised) Classification by the Borrower of a Term              （經修訂）         對包含按要求償還條款

        and maintenance are recognised as an expense in                間內在損益內確認為開支。
        profit or loss during the financial period in which
        they are incurred.

      3.7 Property, plant and equipment                                 3.7 物業、廠房及設備（續）
          (Continued)
          An item of property, plant and equipment is                     物業、廠房及設備項目乃於出售
          derecognised upon disposal or when no future                    後或當預期持續使用該資產將不
          economic benefits are expected to arise from                    會產生未來經濟利益時終止確
          the continued use of the asset. Any gain or loss                認。於終止確認該資產時產生之
          arising on derecognition of the asset (calculated as            任何盈虧（以出售所得款項淨額
          the difference between the net disposal proceeds                與該項目之賬面值間之差額計算）
          and the carrying amount of the item) is included                乃計入於該項目終止確認年度之
          in the profit or loss in the year in which the item is          損益內。
          derecognised.

      3.8 Investment properties                                         3.8 投資物業
          Investment properties are land and/or buildings                 投資物業為根據租賃權益為賺取
          which are owned or held under a leasehold                       租金收入及╱或資本增值而擁有
          interest to earn rental income and/or for capital               或持有的土地及╱或樓宇。
          appreciation.

          On initial recognition, investment property                     投資物業初步確認時按其成本（包
          is measured at cost, including any directly                     括 任 何 直 接 相 關 開 支）計 量。於
          attributable expenditure. Subsequent to initial                 初步確認後，投資物業按公平值
          recognition, investment property is stated at fair              列賬。
          value.

          Gains or losses arising from either changes in the              公平值變動或銷售投資物業所產
          fair value or the sale of an investment property                生之盈虧於其產生期間計入損益
          are included in profit or loss in the period in which           內。
          they arise.

      3.9 Impairment of non-financial assets                            3.9 非金融資產減值
          Goodwill arising on acquisition of subsidiaries,                因收購附屬公司而產生之商譽、
          property, plant and equipment and interests in                  物業、廠房及設備以及於附屬公
          subsidiaries are subject to impairment testing.                 司之權益均須進行減值測試。

          Goodwill is tested for impairment at least annually,            商譽須至少每年進行減值測試一
          irrespective of whether there is any indication                 次，而不論有否跡象顯示出現減
          that they are impaired. All other assets are tested             值。所有其他資產在有跡象顯示
          for impairment whenever there are indications                   未必能收回資產賬面值時進行減
          that the asset’s carrying amount may not be                     值測試。
          recoverable.

     3.9 Impairment of non-financial assets                          3.9 非金融資產減值（續）
        (Continued)
        An impairment loss is recognised as an expense                 減值虧損乃按資產之賬面值超出
        immediately for the amount by which the asset’s                其可收回金額之數額即時確認為
        carrying amount exceeds its recoverable amount.                開支。可收回金額為反映市況之
        Recoverable amount is the higher of fair value,                公平值減出售成本與使用價值兩
        reflecting market conditions less costs of disposal,           者之較高者。評估使用價值時，
        and value in use. In assessing value in use, the               估計未來現金流量採用稅前貼現
        estimated future cash flows are discounted to                  率貼現至其現值，以反映現時市
        their present value using a pre-tax discount rate              場對金錢時間價值及有關資產特
        that reflects current market assessment of time                有風險之評估。
        value of money and the risk specific to the asset.

         For the purposes of assessing impairment, where               就評估減值而言，倘資產產生之
         an asset does not generate cash inflows largely               現金流入大致上並非獨立於其他
         independent from those from other assets,                     資產，可收回金額則按可獨立產
         the recoverable amount is determined for the                  生現金流入之最小資產組別（即
         smallest group of assets that generate cash                   現 金 產 生 單 位）釐 定。因 此，部
         inflows independently (i.e. a cash-generating unit).          分資產乃個別進行減值測試，另
         As a result, some assets are tested individually              有部分按現金產生單位進行測
         for impairment and some are tested at cash-                   試。特別是商譽乃分配至該等預
         generating unit level. Goodwill in particular is              期可受惠於相關業務合併所帶來
         allocated to those cash-generating units that are             協同效應之現金產生單位，即本
         expected to benefit from synergies of the related             集團內就內部管理目的而監控商
         business combination and represent the lowest                 譽之最低水平。
         level within the Group at which the goodwill is
         monitored for internal management purpose.

         Impairment losses recognised for cash-generating              就已獲分配商譽之現金產生單位
         units, to which goodwill has been allocated,                  確認之減值虧損初步計入商譽之
         are credited initially to the carrying amount                 賬面值。任何剩餘減值虧損於現
         of goodwill. Any remaining impairment loss is                 金產生單位內按比例自其他資產
         charged pro rata to the other assets in the cash              扣除，惟資產的賬面值將不會削
         generating unit, except that the carrying value of            減至低於其個別公平值減出售成
         an asset will not be reduced below its individual             本（如可以計量），或使用價值（如
         fair value less costs of disposal, if measurable, or          可以釐定）。
         value in use, if determinable.

         An impairment loss on goodwill is not reversed                商譽減值虧損不會於其後期間
         in subsequent periods. In respect of other                    撥回。就其他非金融資產而言，
         non-financial assets, an impairment loss is                   倘用於釐定資產可收回金額之估
         reversed if there has been a favourable change                計出現有利變動，則撥回減值虧
         in the estimates used to determine the asset’s                損，惟資產之賬面值不得超出在
         recoverable amount and only to the extent that                並未確認減值虧損之情況下原應
         the asset’s carrying amount does not exceed                   釐定之賬面值（扣除折舊或攤銷）
                                                                                     。
         the carrying amount that would have been
         determined, net of depreciation or amortisation, if
         no impairment loss had been recognised.

          end of reporting period.

          differences. Except for goodwill and initial                     認。除商譽及於交易中初步確認

          which is not a business combination and at the                   業務合併，在交易時不影響會計
          time of the transaction affect neither accounting                或應課稅溢利，且不會產生相等
          nor taxable profits and does not give rise to equal              的 應 課 稅 及 可 扣 稅 之 暫 時 差 異）
          taxable and deductible temporary differences,                    外，所有應課稅暫時差異之遞延
          deferred tax liabilities are recognised for all                  稅項負債均會確認。在有可能出
          taxable temporary differences. Deferred tax assets               現可運用可扣稅之暫時差異及稅
          are recognised to the extent that it is probable                 率虧損抵銷應課稅溢利時，遞延
          that taxable profits will be available against which             稅項資產方會確認。遞延稅項乃

          can be utilised. Deferred tax is measured at the                 變現或結算之預期方式及於報告

          The Group mainly operates in Hong Kong, the                      本集團主要於香港、中國及日本

          currencies of respective group companies. The                    及結算。本集團主要就若干以美
          Group’s exposure to foreign currency risk primarily              元（「美 元」）計 值 之 金 融 工 具 面
          arise from certain financial instruments which                   對外幣風險，而美元並非功能貨
          are denominated in United States Dollar (“USD”),                 幣。就此而言，分析假設港元與
          which are currencies other than the functional                   美元間的聯繫匯率不會因美元兌
          currency. In this respect, it is assumed that the                港元的價值出現任何變動而受到
          pegged rate between the HK$ and the USD would                    重大影響。

          The Group currently does not have a foreign                      本集團目前並無外幣對沖政策。
          currency hedging policy.

          Credit risk                                                      信貸風險
          In order to minimise credit risk, the Group has                  為盡量減低信貸風險，本集團已
          policies in place for determination of credit limits,            制定政策釐定信貸限額、信貸批
          credit approvals and other monitoring procedures                 核及其他監控程序，以確保採取
          to ensure that follow-up action is taken to recover              跟進行動收回逾期債項。此外，
          overdue debts. In addition, the Group reviews                    於各報告期末，本集團審閱各個
          the recoverable amount of each individual debtor                 別應收賬項之可收回金額，以確
          at the end of each reporting period to ensure                    保已就不可收回金額作出足夠減
          that adequate impairment losses are made for                     值虧損。就此，本公司董事認為
          irrecoverable amounts. In this regard, the directors             信貸風險已大幅減低。
          of the Company consider that the credit risk is
          significantly reduced.

          The credit risk of the Group is primarily attributable           本集團之信貸風險主要來自貿
          to trade receivables, other receivables and                      易應收賬項、其他應收款項及按

          institution, pledged bank balances and bank                      款、已抵押銀行結餘以及銀行結
          balance and cash.                                                餘及現金。

          there has been a significant increase in credit risk             險是否會持續大幅增加。為評估

          period. To assess whether there is a significant                 團將報告日期資產發生違約的風

          Liquidity risk                                                 流動資金風險
          Individual operating entities within the Group are             本集團內各個別經營實體須負責
          responsible for their own cash management,                     其本身之現金管理，當中包括籌
          including raising of loans to cover expected                   措貸款以應付預期現金需要，倘
          cash demands, subject to approval by the                       借貸高於若干預定授權水平時均
          Company’s board when the borrowings exceed                     須由本公司董事會批准。本集團
          certain predetermined levels of authority. The                 之政策為定期監察其流動資金需
          Group’s policy is to regularly monitor its liquidity           求及遵守借貸契諾，以確保其維
          requirements and its compliance with lending                   持充足現金儲備，並獲主要金融
          covenants, to ensure that it maintains sufficient              機構提供充足融資額之承諾，以
          reserves of cash and adequate committed lines of               應付長短期之流動資金需求。
          funding from major financial institutions to meet

     (b) Fair values                                                 (b) 公平值
          The fair value of listed securities is determined                 上市證券之公平值乃參考其於報

          reporting date.

                    markets for identical assets or                                     活躍市場之報價（未
                    liabilities.                                                        經調整）。

                    included within Level 1 that are                                    價 外，可 直 接 或 間
                    observable for the assets or liabilities,                           接觀察之資產或負
                    either directly or indirectly.                                      債輸入數據。

                    are not based on observable market                                  場數據計算之資產

           and requires different business strategies as                      團個別管理該等分類。該等分類

           between different operating segments for the                       間交易（二零二四年：無）。由於
           year (2024: Nil). Central revenue and expenses are                 中央收益及開支並無計入主要營
           not allocated to the operating segments as they                    運決策者用作評估分類表現之分
           are not included in the measure of the segments’                   類業績計量，故有關收益及開支

    AND DEPOSITS IN A FINANCIAL                                       構之存款
    INSTITUTION

      Bank balances and cash                                        銀行結餘及現金
      Cash at banks earns interest at floating rates based on       銀行現金按每日銀行存款利率之浮動
      daily bank deposit rates. Short term time deposits are        利率賺取利息。短期定期存款之存款
      made for varying periods ranging from one day to three        期介乎一日至三個月不等，視乎本集
      months depending on the immediate cash requirements           團之即時現金需求而定，並按各短期
      of the Group, and earn interest at the respective             定期存款利率賺取利息。銀行結餘存
      short term time deposit rates. The bank balances are          放於信譽良好且最近並無拖欠記錄之
      deposited with creditworthy financial institutions with       金融機構。銀行存款以及銀行結餘及
      no recent history of default. The carrying amounts of the     現金之賬面值與其公平值相若。
      bank deposits and bank balances and cash approximate
      to their fair values.

      Time deposits comprised of short-term bank deposits           定期存款包括本集團所持有原到
      of HK$6,032,000 (2024: HK$10,576,000) with an original        期日為三個月或以下的短期銀行

      amounts carried interest 2.9%–4.5% (2024: 3.1%–4.9%)          10,576,000 港元）。該等金額按年利率

                                                                    4.9 厘）計息。

                                                                    多家銀行。

      RMB is not freely convertible into other currencies.          人民幣不可自由兌換為其他貨幣。根
      Under Mainland China’s Foreign Exchange Control               據中國大陸外匯管制法規及結匯、售

36. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                    36. 資金風險管理

   The Group’s objective of managing capital is to               本集團資金管理目標為確保本集團有
   safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going          能力持續經營，從而為股東帶來回報
   concern in order to provide returns and benefits for          及 利 益，並 維 持 最 理 想 資 本 結 構，以
   members and to maintain an optimal capital structure          減低資本成本。
   to reduce cost of capital.

   The capital structure of the Group consists of net debts,     本集團之資本結構包括負債淨額，當
   which includes borrowings less deposits in a financial        中包括借貸減於金融機構之存款、已
   institution, pledged bank balances, bank balances and         抵押銀行結餘、銀行結餘及現金以及
   cash and total equity as disclosed in the consolidated        於綜合財務狀況表披露之總權益。
   statement of financial position.

   The Group’s management reviews the capital structure          本集團管理層每半年檢討資本結構一
   on a semi-annual basis. As part of this review, the           次。作 為 此 檢 討 其 中 部 分，管 理 層 考
   management considers the cost of capital and the              慮各類別資本之資本成本及相關風險。
   risks associated with each class of capital. Based on         按照管理層之建議，本集團將透過發
   recommendations of the management, the Group will             行新股及發行新債權或贖回現有債權，
   balance its overall capital structure through new share       以平衡其整體資本結構。
   issues as well as the issue of new debts or redemption
   of existing debts.
